en:{A06A9A}1. ATHEISTS {E3E3E3}(The Rationalists / No Faith)\nWe reject the supernatural and believe that the world operates through natural laws, science, and reason. There is no divine force guiding us - we are responsible for our own destiny and morality. Spirituality is not needed to be ethical or fulfilled; compassion and critical thinking are enough. The universe doesn't care about us, which is why we must create our own meaning and protect each other through logic and evidence. Faith is comforting but it blinds people from truth.\n\n{A06A9A}2. THE ASCENDANTS {E3E3E3}(Modern church)\nWe believe that humanity is constantly evolving toward a higher form of existence, beyond material and traditional limits. Spirituality is not found in the past or in religion, but in progress and personal transcendence. The future belongs to those who understand that human consciousness can be elevated through knowledge, technology, and enlightenment. The world is a universe in constant upgrade.\n\n{A06A9A}3. THE HOLY ORDER {E3E3E3}(Medieval church)\nOur faith is rooted in tradition, sacrifice, and devotion to eternal values. The world is full of evil and temptation, but through asceticism and pure faith you can achieve spiritual salvation. Honor, loyalty, and respect for divine law are the moral pillars of existence. The future is built on the strong foundations of the past.\n\n{A06A9A}4. THE DIVINE BLESSING {E3E3E3}(Santa Luz - Hispanic/Colonial church)\nWe are connected with nature, with community, and with the supreme good that watches over us. Spirituality lives in honest work, in love for our fellow man, and in respect for the sacred that surrounds us. Our beliefs are simple but powerful - goodness, compassion, and harmony with the earth are the path to peace. The world is a blessing, not a battle.
ro:{A06A9A}1. ATEII {E3E3E3}(Rationalistii / Fara credinta)\nRespingem supranaturalul si credem ca lumea functioneaza prin legi naturale, stiinta si ratiune. Nu exista o forta divina care sa ne ghideze - suntem responsabili pentru propriul nostru destin si pentru propria moralitate. Nu ai nevoie de spiritualitate ca sa fii etic sau implinit; compasiunea si gandirea critica sunt de ajuns. Universului nu ii pasa de noi, tocmai de aceea trebuie sa ne cream singuri sensul si sa ne protejam unii pe altii prin logica si dovezi. Credinta este alinatoare, dar ii orbeste pe oameni fata de adevar.\n\n{A06A9A}2. THE ASCENDANTS {E3E3E3}(Biserica moderna)\nCredem ca omenirea evolueaza constant catre o forma superioara de existenta, dincolo de limitele materiale si traditionale. Spiritualitatea nu se gaseste in trecut sau in religie, ci in progres si in transcendenta personala. Viitorul apartine celor care inteleg ca constiinta umana poate fi inaltata prin cunoastere, tehnologie si iluminare. Lumea este un univers aflat intr-un upgrade continuu.\n\n{A06A9A}3. THE HOLY ORDER {E3E3E3}(Biserica medievala)\nCredinta noastra este inradacinata in traditie, sacrificiu si devotament fata de valorile eterne. Lumea este plina de rau si de ispita, dar prin ascetism si credinta curata poti dobandi mantuirea spirituala. Onoarea, loialitatea si respectul fata de legea divina sunt pilonii morali ai existentei. Viitorul se cladeste pe temeliile solide ale trecutului.\n\n{A06A9A}4. THE DIVINE BLESSING {E3E3E3}(Santa Luz - biserica hispanica/coloniala)\nSuntem conectati cu natura, cu comunitatea si cu binele suprem care vegheaza asupra noastra. Spiritualitatea traieste in munca cinstita, in dragostea fata de aproapele si in respectul pentru sacrul care ne inconjoara. Credintele noastre sunt simple, dar puternice - bunatatea, compasiunea si armonia cu pamantul sunt calea catre pace. Lumea este o binecuvantare, nu o batalie.

Get in a depot bus, then /bus [1-3] to pick a line and start.\nDrive to each marked stop: you earn $300 + $1/meter driven per stop reached.\nReaching the last stop pays an extra $1.000 bonus, then the route loops back to stop 1 automatically.\nPassengers who get in your bus while you're on duty are charged a $100 ticket automatically.\nYour current stop/earnings show in the job textdraw, not in chat.\n\nCommands: /getjob 7, /bus [1-3], /stopwork.

en:{E3E3E3}Truck Driver - resupply bars and shops.\n\nTake it with /getjob 14. Get in one of the 2 job trucks and attach one of the 2 job trailers (see /joblist), then /job to start.\nFirst pick what you're hauling: Drinks (bars), Electronics, Tools or 24/7 Goods. You're sent to a random loading point out of 3; drive there to load to 100% (3 seconds), then pick a destination of the matching type from the list (shows stock level and distance) and deliver to it.\nDelivery has spillage: the truck loses 2 units of cargo for every 1 unit that actually reaches the destination, so a full load can resupply at most half of what's missing. If it's already full, you'll be asked to pick another one. Pays $6.000-$7.500 per run, then the run ends - use /job again for another run.\n\nCommands: /getjob 14, /job, /stopwork, /joblist.

Follow the checkpoints.\n\nUse /howto job [1-20] for a specific job.

Urmareste checkpoint-urile.\n\nFoloseste /howto job [1-20] pentru un job anume.

Only on-duty officers have police powers.\n- /wanted [player] [0-6] [reason] - set a suspect's wanted level\n- /arrest [player] - jail a wanted suspect (at an arrest zone)\n- /cuff [player] - handcuff a suspect sitting in the same police vehicle as you (rank 1+): they can't leave the car until you use /cuff again to uncuff. Arrested suspects are uncuffed automatically\n- /m [player] - megaphone: order a driver to pull over (in a faction vehicle, within 50m)\n- /radar install [limit] - set up a speed radar and sanction speeders (/radar remove)\n- /garage, /entrace - move around the station; press H near the LSPD barrier to open it\n\n{FFFF00}Vehicle checks:{E3E3E3} police can inspect papers and confiscate:\n- /checklicenses [player], /suspendlic\n- /confiscate insurance [player] (rank 2+)\n- /confiscate licence [A/B/C/D/P/H/all] [player] - driving + airplane (P) & helicopter (H) licenses\n\n{FFFF00}Self-surrender:{E3E3E3} at the [ Jail ] point, if no officer is on duty, a wanted player (level 2+) can turn themselves in with /arrestme - same jail time & fine as /arrest, but +20%, paid straight into the Police vault.

en:{E3E3E3}Mafia - organized crime family.\n\nMafias run drugs and fight over territory.\n- /drugs transport - haul weed to the vault\n- /drugs craft - turn weed into drugs\n- /drugs get / use - take & use drugs during a war\n- /seif - the faction vault (rank 4+)\n- /equip - get weapons inside the HQ\n- /smuggle - run contraband by boat: drive a designated boat (plate SMG) to the loading point (10s freeze), then to 2 random collection points (5s freeze each). At loading, Police are alerted and everyone aboard gets wanted 6. Finally, switch to your faction's Sultan (driver or passenger) and deliver it to a random hotel: wanted drops to 5, the vault gets $25.000 + 500g weed, and each living crew member earns $2.000-3.000. The Police get a bounty for every smuggler they catch. One successful run per faction per day.\n- /war - start a turf war\n- /warsurrender - surrender an ongoing war (rank 4+)\nEach mafia controls its own territories.
ro:{E3E3E3}Mafia - familie de crima organizata.\n\nMafiile se ocupa de droguri si se lupta pe teritorii.\n- /drugs transport - transporti iarba la seif\n- /drugs craft - transformi iarba in droguri\n- /drugs get / use - iei si folosesti droguri in timpul unui razboi\n- /seif - seiful factiunii (rank 4+)\n- /equip - iei arme din sediu\n- /smuggle - contrabanda pe mare: condu o barca desemnata (numar SMG) la punctul de incarcare (10s freeze), apoi la 2 puncte de colectare aleatorii (5s freeze fiecare). La incarcare, Politia e alertata si toti cei de la bord primesc wanted 6. La final, treci in Sultan-ul factiunii (sofer sau pasager) si livreaza la un hotel aleatoriu: wanted scade la 5, seiful primeste $25.000 + 500g iarba, iar fiecare membru in viata castiga $2.000-3.000. Politia primeste o prima pentru fiecare contrabandist prins. O cursa reusita per factiune pe zi.\n- /war - pornesti un razboi de teritoriu\n- /warsurrender - te predai intr-un razboi in desfasurare (rank 4+)\nFiecare mafie isi controleaza propriile teritorii.

en:{E3E3E3}Loco Outlaw Customs - the city's tuning shop. Not a mafia - no smuggling, no turf wars.\n\nMembers can install parts on ANY player's vehicle (not just their own), for free, at one of the faction's tuning points.\n\n{FFFF00}/tune requirements:{E3E3E3}\n- You must be the driver of the target vehicle\n- Engine off ({A06A9A}/changecar engine{E3E3E3}) and hood open ({A06A9A}/changecar hood{E3E3E3})\n- Within range of a Loco Outlaw Customs tuning point\n\nUse /tune to open the parts menu: Spoiler, Hood, Roof, Side Skirt, Front/Rear Bumper, Exhaust, Wheels, Nitro, Hydraulics, Paintjob, or Remove All Mods.

en:{E3E3E3}A hotel is an ownable property (like a house) where {A06A9A}any{E3E3E3} player can pay to sleep - no bed and no ownership required. It's a passive income property: guests pay, the owner collects. You can own only ONE hotel at a time.\n\n{FFFF00}What you can do AT a hotel (anyone):{E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}/sleep{E3E3E3} - rent a room for {A06A9A}$150{E3E3E3}. You freeze for {A06A9A}45 seconds{E3E3E3}, then your tiredness drops to {A06A9A}10{E3E3E3}.\n  It does NOT have to be your own hotel, and you don't need a bed. Just stand at the hotel pickup.\n  Compared to sleeping at home (free, 30s, tiredness -> 0), a hotel is the paid option when you're away from your house or don't own a bed.\n- {A06A9A}/buyhotel{E3E3E3} - buy the hotel you're standing on, if it's for sale\n\n{FFFF00}Buying & selling:{E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}/buyhotel{E3E3E3} - buy the hotel whose pickup you're standing on (sold by the server or another player)\n- {A06A9A}/sellhotel [price]{E3E3E3} - list your hotel for sale at that price; the money goes to your bank when someone buys it\n- {A06A9A}/sellhotel 0{E3E3E3} - cancel the listing\n- {A06A9A}/sellhotelstate{E3E3E3} - sell it back to the state instantly for {A06A9A}60%{E3E3E3} of its value\n\n{FFFF00}Renting a room long-term:{E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}/renthotel{E3E3E3} - rent a nearby hotel an admin marked rentable (not the same as {A06A9A}/sleep{E3E3E3})\nBlocked if you own a house or already rent something. Rent is taken from you every PayDay, into the hotel's bank.\n- {A06A9A}/endrent{E3E3E3} - give up your current rent\n\n{FFFF00}Info & navigation:{E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}/htlstats{E3E3E3} - name, ID, price, hotel bank balance and the sleep price\n- {A06A9A}/findmy hotel{E3E3E3} - set a checkpoint to the hotel you own\n- {A06A9A}/findhotel [id]{E3E3E3} - set a checkpoint to any hotel by its ID\n\n{FFFF00}Owner income:{E3E3E3} every time a guest uses {A06A9A}/sleep{E3E3E3} at your hotel, {A06A9A}$75{E3E3E3} (half the $150 they pay) goes into the {A06A9A}hotel's bank{E3E3E3}; the other half is lost to running costs. This happens whether you are online or not.\n- {A06A9A}/mybank hotel withdraw [amount]{E3E3E3} - take the earnings out (near a bank/ATM)\n- {A06A9A}/mybank hotel deposit [amount]{E3E3E3} - put money in\n- {A06A9A}/mybank{E3E3E3} - see the balance of every property you own\n\n{FFFF00}Tip:{E3E3E3} a hotel near a busy area earns more, because tiredness builds up on everyone ({A06A9A}+2/minute{E3E3E3}) and players away from home need somewhere to sleep. See {A06A9A}/howto house{E3E3E3} for the full tiredness rules.

en:{E3E3E3}A caravan is a trailer you tow behind your own car and can camp at, turning any spot into a temporary spawn point. You must {A06A9A}own a caravan{E3E3E3} - an admin gives you one.\n\n{FFFF00}Commands:{E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}/attach{E3E3E3} - hook the caravan to your PERSONAL vehicle. You must be {A06A9A}driving{E3E3E3} that vehicle and be near the caravan.\n- {A06A9A}/detach{E3E3E3} - unhook it and park it where you stand. The spot is {A06A9A}saved{E3E3E3} - the caravan reappears there after a restart.\n- {A06A9A}/findmycaravan{E3E3E3} - set a checkpoint to your parked caravan\n- {A06A9A}/camp{E3E3E3} - start camping. You must be {A06A9A}driving{E3E3E3} with the caravan {A06A9A}attached{E3E3E3}.\n\n{FFFF00}Towing:{E3E3E3} while attached, the caravan follows your car. Park on {A06A9A}flat ground{E3E3E3} before /detach, otherwise it can end up tilted or stuck.\n\n{FFFF00}Camping - what /camp actually does:{E3E3E3} your {A06A9A}SPAWN POINT{E3E3E3} moves to the caravan's spot for {A06A9A}3 hours{E3E3E3} (= {A06A9A}3 paydays{E3E3E3}, since payday runs once an hour). Every time you die or reconnect you respawn at the camp instead of your normal spawn.\nWhen it expires, your spawn resets to normal automatically (house, faction or civilian, whatever you had).\n\nThis is handy for setting up far from the city - drag the caravan somewhere remote, /camp, and you stop losing time travelling back after every death.
ro:{E3E3E3}Rulota e o remorca pe care o tractezi in spatele masinii tale si la care poti campa, transformand orice loc intr-un punct temporar de spawn. Trebuie sa {A06A9A}detii o rulota{E3E3E3} - ti-o da un admin.\n\n{FFFF00}Comenzi:{E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}/attach{E3E3E3} - atasezi rulota la vehiculul tau PERSONAL. Trebuie sa {A06A9A}conduci{E3E3E3} acel vehicul si sa fii langa rulota.\n- {A06A9A}/detach{E3E3E3} - o detasezi si o parchezi acolo unde stai. Locul e {A06A9A}salvat{E3E3E3} - rulota reapare acolo dupa un restart.\n- {A06A9A}/findmycaravan{E3E3E3} - pui un checkpoint la rulota parcata\n- {A06A9A}/camp{E3E3E3} - incepi sa campezi. Trebuie sa {A06A9A}conduci{E3E3E3} cu rulota {A06A9A}atasata{E3E3E3}.\n\n{FFFF00}Tractare:{E3E3E3} cat e atasata, rulota urmeaza masina. Parcheaza pe {A06A9A}teren plat{E3E3E3} inainte de /detach, altfel poate ramane inclinata sau blocata.\n\n{FFFF00}Camparea - ce face de fapt /camp:{E3E3E3} {A06A9A}PUNCTUL TAU DE SPAWN{E3E3E3} se muta la rulota pentru {A06A9A}3 ore{E3E3E3} (= {A06A9A}3 paydays{E3E3E3}, pentru ca payday-ul ruleaza o data pe ora). De fiecare data cand mori sau te reconectezi, faci spawn la tabara in loc de spawn-ul normal.\nCand expira, spawn-ul revine automat la normal (casa, factiune sau civil, ce aveai).\n\nE util cand te stabilesti departe de oras - duci rulota undeva izolat, dai /camp, si nu mai pierzi timp cu drumul inapoi dupa fiecare moarte.

O verifici cu {A06A9A}/foreststats{E3E3E3}.\n\n{FFFF00}Copacii:{E3E3E3} fiecare padure are {A06A9A}currentTree/maxTree{E3E3E3}. Creste cu {A06A9A}+10 la fiecare PayDay{E3E3E3} (plafonat la maxim) - astia sunt copacii pe care ii taie Lumberjack-ii cu {A06A9A}/chopwood{E3E3E3}.\n\n{FFFF00}Puieti (copaci mai repede):{E3E3E3}\n- Cumperi puieti la un {A06A9A}Tools Shop{E3E3E3} ({A06A9A}$10/bucata{E3E3E3}) stand intr-un {A06A9A}Benson{E3E3E3} - o sa fii intrebat cati vrei.\n- Condu Benson-ul pana la padurea ta si foloseste {A06A9A}/forest saplings deposit [cantitate]{E3E3E3} ca sa ii depozitezi (maxim {A06A9A}2x maxTree{E3E3E3}).\n- Un Lumberjack poate apoi planta unul: condu un {A06A9A}Forklift{E3E3E3} pana la padure si foloseste {A06A9A}/forest tree plant{E3E3E3}. Apare un marcaj in padure - coboara, treci pe {A06A9A}Lopata{E3E3E3} si apasa {A06A9A}FIRE{E3E3E3} ca sa plantezi (plateste {A06A9A}$600{E3E3E3}).\n- Puietii plantati se adauga la {A06A9A}currentTree{E3E3E3} la urmatorul PayDay.

en:{E3E3E3}Pick a faith, pray at church and confess your sins every Sunday.\n\n{FFFF00}Your religion:{E3E3E3} choose one with {A06A9A}/setreligion{E3E3E3} - Ateu, The Ascendants, The Holy Order or The Divine Blessing. Read what each one believes with {A06A9A}/whatreligion{E3E3E3}. There are 3 churches on the map, listed in your GPS.\n\n{FFFF00}The 12 sins:{E3E3E3} see the full list with {A06A9A}/sinlist{E3E3E3}. You never tick them yourself - they are flagged automatically as you play (getting wanted, robbing, gambling, smuggling, killing out of revenge, speeding past a radar, jail time, drinking or drugs, eating meat on Wednesday/Friday, driving without a license, riding along with an illegal /tune).\nSin 4 (Anti-Nature) counts as guilty from the start - plant a tree or do one Water Hauler shift to clear it. The whole table resets every Monday at 00:00.\n\n{FFFF00}Praying:{E3E3E3} {A06A9A}/pray{E3E3E3} once per day. Every prayer is counted for that week.\n\n{FFFF00}Sunday confession:{E3E3E3} on Sunday {A06A9A}/pray{E3E3E3} also opens a panel with the 12 sins. Click the ones you think you committed (they turn green - click again to untick), then press {A06A9A}ASK FORGIVENESS{E3E3E3}. You are frozen for 5 seconds plus 1 per confessed sin while the priest prays.\n\n{FFFF00}Rewards:{E3E3E3} you always get a prayer bonus of {A06A9A}$5.000 per prayer{E3E3E3} that week plus a small random amount. Everything below needs at least {A06A9A}4 prayers{E3E3E3} that week:\n- wrong on 2+ sins: HP 100, tiredness 0, sickness cured\n- wrong on 1 sin: the above, personal vehicles cleaned and {A06A9A}$25.000{E3E3E3}\n- all 12 correct: the above, full fuel in every personal vehicle, {A06A9A}+1 day on every license{E3E3E3} (an expired one becomes valid again today) and {A06A9A}$50.000{E3E3E3}\n\n{FFFF00}Weekly quests:{E3E3E3} each religion has its own 2 weekly quests (tied to its beliefs - e.g. The Divine Blessing rewards planting trees and watering plants, The Ascendants reward tuning and flying Crop Duster runs). Check your progress and claim rewards with {A06A9A}/quest{E3E3E3}. They also reset every Monday at 00:00.
ro:

en:{E3E3E3}Part 2 of 2. Page one ({A06A9A}/howto setquest{E3E3E3}) covers building the quest.\n\n{FFFF00}== EVERY PLAYER HAS HIS OWN COPY =={E3E3E3}\nObjects are created per player, not once for the server. What one player picks up stays on the map for everybody else, and never comes back for him - each point counts once per player.\nNobody can empty the map for others, nobody races anyone for a pickup, and every player can finish the quest in full. A pickup is remembered in the database, so it stays collected through relogs and restarts.\n\n{FFFF00}== WHAT THE PLAYER SEES =={E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/quest{E3E3E3} opens one dialog: the weekly religion quests first, then a section named after the holiday. Each line shows the quest name and the progress ({FFFF00}7/10{E3E3E3}), turning green when finished.\nWalking into an object gives a chat message and adds 1 to the progress. The reward is paid the moment the player opens /quest with the target reached - money plus a confirmation line. It is paid once and never again.\n\n{FFFF00}== THE 4 STATES =={E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}start{E3E3E3}: objects on the map, progress counts, quests listed\n- {A06A9A}pause{E3E3E3}: objects removed, progress and listing stay (quests show {FFD700}[PAUSE]{E3E3E3})\n- {A06A9A}stop{E3E3E3}: objects removed, no new progress, still listed (quests show {FF5D5D}[DISABLED]{E3E3E3})\n- {A06A9A}hide{E3E3E3}: nothing loaded at all, as if no event existed\n{A06A9A}/holiday [state]{E3E3E3} sets it for everything; {A06A9A}/holiday quest [state] [key]{E3E3E3} sets it for one quest only. The holiday is the ceiling - the lower of the two wins, so a quest on start stays paused while the holiday is paused. Use it to run two quests and keep a third hidden until you are done placing its points.\nPlayers are announced only when the event becomes visible or stops being visible - moving between start, pause and stop is silent.\n\n{FFFF00}== IF SOMETHING LOOKS BROKEN =={E3E3E3}\n- {FFFF00}one quest missing from /quest{E3E3E3}: that quest is on hide - {A06A9A}/holiday quest{E3E3E3} shows every state\n- {FFFF00}all quests missing from /quest{E3E3E3}: the holiday state is hide\n- {FFFF00}no objects on the map{E3E3E3}: state is not start, or you are in a different interior / virtual world than where you saved them\n- {FFFF00}progress not rising{E3E3E3}: state is pause or stop, or the quest type is not collect\n- {FFFF00}nobody can finish it{E3E3E3}: the target is higher than the number of points placed - points do not come back, so target must be <= points\n- {FFFF00}reward did not come again{E3E3E3}: it is paid once per player, {A06A9A}/holiday reset{E3E3E3} clears that for everyone\n- {FFFF00}edited something, nothing changed{E3E3E3}: the world reloads only while the event is running\n\n{FFFF00}== LIMITS =={E3E3E3}\nUp to {FFFF00}16{E3E3E3} quests and {FFFF00}150{E3E3E3} object points; {A06A9A}/holiday obj list{E3E3E3} shows the first 40.\n\n{FFFF00}== MOVING TO THE NEXT HOLIDAY =={E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday stop{E3E3E3}, {A06A9A}/holiday name Christmas{E3E3E3}, {A06A9A}/holiday reset{E3E3E3} (wipes progress so an old key can be reused), delete the old quests and objects, add the new ones, {A06A9A}/holiday start{E3E3E3}.\n\nFull command reference: {A06A9A}/holiday{E3E3E3}.
ro:{E3E3E3}Partea 2 din 2. Prima pagina ({A06A9A}/howto setquest{E3E3E3}) arata cum construiesti quest-ul.\n\n{FFFF00}== FIECARE JUCATOR ARE PROPRIA COPIE =={E3E3E3}\nObiectele se creeaza per jucator, nu o singura data pentru tot serverul. Ce ridica un jucator ramane pe harta pentru toti ceilalti si nu ii mai reapare lui - fiecare punct conteaza o singura data per jucator.\nNimeni nu poate goli harta pentru altii, nimeni nu se bate pe un obiect si fiecare jucator poate termina quest-ul complet. Ridicarea se retine in baza de date, deci ramane luata si dupa relog sau restart.\n\n{FFFF00}== CE VEDE JUCATORUL =={E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/quest{E3E3E3} deschide un singur dialog: intai quest-urile saptamanale de religie, apoi o sectiune cu numele sarbatorii. Fiecare linie arata numele quest-ului si progresul ({FFFF00}7/10{E3E3E3}), verde cand e terminat.\nCand intra intr-un obiect primeste un mesaj si progresul creste cu 1. Recompensa se plateste in clipa in care deschide /quest cu tinta atinsa - bani plus un mesaj de confirmare. Se da o singura data, niciodata a doua oara.\n\n{FFFF00}== CELE 4 STARI =={E3E3E3}\n- {A06A9A}start{E3E3E3}: obiecte pe harta, progresul creste, quest-urile se vad\n- {A06A9A}pause{E3E3E3}: obiectele dispar, progresul si lista raman (quest-urile arata {FFD700}[PAUZA]{E3E3E3})\n- {A06A9A}stop{E3E3E3}: obiectele dispar, progresul nu mai creste, dar lista ramane (arata {FF5D5D}[OPRIT]{E3E3E3})\n- {A06A9A}hide{E3E3E3}: nu se incarca nimic, ca si cum nu ar exista niciun eveniment\n{A06A9A}/holiday [stare]{E3E3E3} o pune pentru tot; {A06A9A}/holiday quest [stare] [cheie]{E3E3E3} o pune doar pentru un quest. Sarbatoarea e plafonul - castiga cea mai mica dintre cele doua, deci un quest pe start ramane in pauza cat timp sarbatoarea e in pauza. Foloseste asta ca sa tii doua quest-uri pornite si al treilea ascuns pana termini de pus punctele lui.\nJucatorii sunt anuntati doar cand evenimentul devine vizibil sau nu mai e vizibil - trecerile intre start, pause si stop sunt tacute.\n\n{FFFF00}== DACA CEVA PARE STRICAT =={E3E3E3}\n- {FFFF00}un singur quest lipseste din /quest{E3E3E3}: quest-ul ala e pe hide - {A06A9A}/holiday quest{E3E3E3} arata toate starile\n- {FFFF00}toate quest-urile lipsesc din /quest{E3E3E3}: starea sarbatorii e hide\n- {FFFF00}niciun obiect pe harta{E3E3E3}: starea nu e start, sau esti in alt interior / virtual world decat cel in care le-ai salvat\n- {FFFF00}progresul nu creste{E3E3E3}: starea e pause sau stop, sau tipul quest-ului nu e collect\n- {FFFF00}nimeni nu poate termina{E3E3E3}: tinta e mai mare decat numarul de puncte puse - punctele nu reapar, deci tinta trebuie sa fie <= puncte\n- {FFFF00}recompensa nu a mai venit{E3E3E3}: se da o singura data per jucator, {A06A9A}/holiday reset{E3E3E3} sterge asta pentru toti\n- {FFFF00}ai editat ceva si nu s-a schimbat nimic{E3E3E3}: lumea se reincarca doar cat timp evenimentul ruleaza\n\n{FFFF00}== LIMITE =={E3E3E3}\nMaxim {FFFF00}16{E3E3E3} quest-uri si {FFFF00}150{E3E3E3} puncte cu obiecte; {A06A9A}/holiday obj list{E3E3E3} arata primele 40.\n\n{FFFF00}== TRECEREA LA URMATOAREA SARBATOARE =={E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday stop{E3E3E3}, {A06A9A}/holiday name Craciun{E3E3E3}, {A06A9A}/holiday reset{E3E3E3} (sterge progresul, ca sa poti refolosi o cheie veche), stergi quest-urile si obiectele vechi, le adaugi pe cele noi, {A06A9A}/holiday start{E3E3E3}.\n\nLista completa de comenzi: {A06A9A}/holiday{E3E3E3}.

en:{E3E3E3}There is ONE holiday event. You rename it each season and give it as many quests as you want. Everything is data in the {A06A9A}event_*{E3E3E3} tables - nothing needs a recompile.\nPart 2: {A06A9A}/howto setquest 2{E3E3E3} (what players see, states, troubleshooting).\n\n{FFFF00}== WHAT A QUEST IS MADE OF =={E3E3E3}\n- {FFFF00}key{E3E3E3}: internal id like {FFFF00}pumpkin_hunt{E3E3E3}. Never shown to players, but every other command needs it. No spaces. Objects and progress are stored under it, so changing it would orphan them - pick it once and keep it.\n- {FFFF00}type{E3E3E3}: the mechanic. {FFFF00}collect{E3E3E3} (pick objects up off the map) is the only one that plays right now; checkpoint / costume / survival / visit are stored but do nothing yet.\n- {FFFF00}target{E3E3E3}: how many pickups finish it.\n- {FFFF00}reward{E3E3E3}: money, paid {FFFF00}once{E3E3E3}, the first time the player opens /quest after reaching the target.\n- {FFFF00}name{E3E3E3} and {FFFF00}description{E3E3E3}: the only parts players read. Change them any time.\n\n{FFFF00}== A COMPLETE EXAMPLE =={E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday name Halloween{E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday quest add pumpkin_hunt collect 10 25000 Pumpkin Hunt{E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday quest desc pumpkin_hunt Collect 10 pumpkins hidden around Los Santos.{E3E3E3}\nthen walk to each spot and {A06A9A}/holiday obj add pumpkin_hunt 19320{E3E3E3} (about 25 times)\n{A06A9A}/holiday start{E3E3E3}\nRe-running {FFFF00}quest add{E3E3E3} with the same key {FFFF00}updates{E3E3E3} it instead of making a second one, so it doubles as the edit command.\n\n{FFFF00}== PLACING THE OBJECTS =={E3E3E3}\nStand exactly where you want it, face the direction you want it turned, then {A06A9A}/holiday obj add [key] [model]{E3E3E3}. Saved automatically: position, facing angle (becomes the rotation), interior and virtual world.\nThat last part matters - an object saved inside a building is only visible to players in that same interior. Save outdoor points while standing outdoors.\nModels sit differently on the ground; some sink, some float. Use {A06A9A}/holiday obj goto [id]{E3E3E3} to jump back and look, {A06A9A}/holiday obj del [id]{E3E3E3} if it is wrong, {A06A9A}/holiday obj model [id] [model]{E3E3E3} to swap the model keeping the coordinates.\n\n{FFFF00}== HOW MANY POINTS =={E3E3E3}\nA point is collected {FFFF00}once per player and never comes back{E3E3E3}. So you need {FFFF00}at least as many points as the target{E3E3E3} - with target 10 and only 8 points nobody can ever finish. Place a few extra so players are not forced to find every single one.\n\nContinue with {A06A9A}/howto setquest 2{E3E3E3}.
ro:{E3E3E3}Exista UN SINGUR eveniment de sarbatoare. Il redenumesti in fiecare sezon si ii pui cate quest-uri vrei. Totul sunt date in tabelele {A06A9A}event_*{E3E3E3} - nu trebuie recompilat nimic.\nPartea 2: {A06A9A}/howto setquest 2{E3E3E3} (ce vad jucatorii, starile, probleme).\n\n{FFFF00}== DIN CE E FACUT UN QUEST =={E3E3E3}\n- {FFFF00}cheie{E3E3E3}: id intern, de exemplu {FFFF00}pumpkin_hunt{E3E3E3}. Nu se arata jucatorilor, dar toate celelalte comenzi au nevoie de el. Fara spatii. Obiectele si progresul se salveaza pe cheia asta, deci daca o schimbi raman orfane - alege-o o data si pastreaz-o.\n- {FFFF00}tip{E3E3E3}: mecanica. {FFFF00}collect{E3E3E3} (ridici obiecte de pe harta) e singura care se joaca acum; checkpoint / costume / survival / visit se salveaza dar inca nu fac nimic.\n- {FFFF00}tinta{E3E3E3}: cate obiecte trebuie stranse ca sa fie gata.\n- {FFFF00}recompensa{E3E3E3}: bani, dati {FFFF00}o singura data{E3E3E3}, prima oara cand jucatorul deschide /quest dupa ce a atins tinta.\n- {FFFF00}nume{E3E3E3} si {FFFF00}descriere{E3E3E3}: singurele parti pe care le citesc jucatorii. Le poti schimba oricand.\n\n{FFFF00}== UN EXEMPLU COMPLET =={E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday name Halloween{E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday quest add pumpkin_hunt collect 10 25000 Vanatoare de dovleci{E3E3E3}\n{A06A9A}/holiday quest desc pumpkin_hunt Aduna 10 dovleci ascunsi prin Los Santos.{E3E3E3}\napoi mergi in fiecare loc si {A06A9A}/holiday obj add pumpkin_hunt 19320{E3E3E3} (cam de 25 de ori)\n{A06A9A}/holiday start{E3E3E3}\nDaca rulezi {FFFF00}quest add{E3E3E3} din nou cu aceeasi cheie, quest-ul se {FFFF00}actualizeaza{E3E3E3} in loc sa se creeze al doilea - deci merge si pe post de comanda de editare.\n\n{FFFF00}== CUM PUI OBIECTELE =={E3E3E3}\nStai exact unde vrei sa fie, uita-te in directia in care vrei sa fie intors, apoi {A06A9A}/holiday obj add [cheie] [model]{E3E3E3}. Se salveaza automat: pozitia, unghiul (devine rotatia), interiorul si virtual world-ul.\nUltima parte conteaza - un obiect salvat intr-o cladire se vede doar de jucatorii din acelasi interior. Punctele de afara salveaza-le stand afara.\nModelele stau diferit pe sol; unele intra in pamant, altele plutesc. Foloseste {A06A9A}/holiday obj goto [id]{E3E3E3} ca sa te intorci si sa vezi, {A06A9A}/holiday obj del [id]{E3E3E3} daca e gresit, {A06A9A}/holiday obj model [id] [model]{E3E3E3} ca sa schimbi modelul pastrand coordonatele.\n\n{FFFF00}== CATE PUNCTE =={E3E3E3}\nUn punct se ridica {FFFF00}o singura data per jucator si nu mai reapare{E3E3E3}. Deci ai nevoie de {FFFF00}cel putin atatea puncte cata e tinta{E3E3E3} - cu tinta 10 si doar 8 puncte nimeni nu poate termina. Pune cateva in plus, ca jucatorii sa nu fie obligati sa le gaseasca chiar pe toate.\n\nContinua cu {A06A9A}/howto setquest 2{E3E3E3}.
